Structure: `[anti-pattern with visceral verb: "X falls apart the moment Y"] , [so-therefore modern-practice: "so you [technique] with [tool-A]"] [and-amplifier: "and let [tool-B] drive every [concept] through [tool-C]"].` Voice: hard-won practitioner wisdom, teaches by contrast. Signature move: the **anti-pattern opening** (`Clicking through a console falls apart the moment you need to rebuild`) — establishes shared pain BEFORE prescribing the solution. Recommend LLM writer alternate paragraph-formula between `foundation-with-differentiator` (slide 78), `imperative-with-platform-abstraction` (slide 79), and `antipattern-then-modern-stack` (this slide) — three distinct voices keep the carousel from monotony.
- **Dashboard-mockup content contract:** show a REAL-looking dashboard state with plausible-app-name + all-child-rows-passing OR one-row-failing (for anti-pattern illustration). Include mint `Synced/Healthy` pills as trust signal. LLM writer emits `dashboard.appName`, `dashboard.subtitle`, `dashboard.statusPills[]`, `dashboard.tree.children[]`; renderer handles styling.
- **Anti-pattern paragraph → all-green dashboard pairing:** the paragraph criticizes console-clicking; the dashboard shows the outcome of doing it right (all-synced). **Reinforcement device:** the paragraph is "here's what NOT to do"; the visual is "here's what happens when you DO do it right". Recommend LLM writer coordinate `paragraph.tone: "antipattern-callout"` with `dashboard.state: "all-healthy"` — the visual is the positive counterexample to the negative paragraph.

  - This is a more complete arc than the earlier VPC carousel finding (which lacked a mid-payoff slide). Suggests nextwork's 11-slide format has ~2 possible arcs:
    - **VPC / educational:** cover → concept-contrast → parts-of-X → deep-dives → reveal → close-CTA.
    - **Roadmap / progression:** cover → 7 stage bodies → payoff-synthesis → recap → close-CTA (2 close slides instead of 1).
  - LLM writer contract: expose `carousel.narrativeArc: "educational" | "progression"` — determines the slot structure.
- **BOOKEND-PAYOFF PATTERN is a first-observed nextwork narrative device.** The cover promises `$128k → $205k`; the payoff slide delivers "here it is again, at the end, still true". Reader gets emotional closure that the promise wasn't hype. Recommend our LLM writer emit `slide.reprisesCoverHero: boolean` for any slide that reuses the cover's hero-visualization.
- **The "context-adaptive text colour" pattern is well-established now**: slides 78-84 used dark text on grass; slide 85 uses white text on sky (title) + white text on grass (paragraph + citation). The mid-slide chart labels are white (on sky). This is a **single design principle at work**: text colour = whatever gives best contrast against the artwork region directly behind it. Recommend renderer helper `getSlideTextColor({y, sliceArtwork})` — samples the artwork's average luminance at the text's y-range and returns white or dark accordingly. LLM writer just emits `text.color: "auto"` and the renderer handles it.
- **Corpus totals:** analyzed 85 slides across 4 folders (others 1-11, SahilBloom 12-22, claude 23-47, nextwork 48-85). Slide 83 is a duplicate of slide 80, so **84 unique slides**. Nextwork corpus alone = 38 files, 37 unique slides. **Total time on task:** ~30-40 hours of analysis represented in the two `SLIDE_REFERENCES_FULL*.md` files.
- **NEXTWORK CAROUSELS FULLY MAPPED (5 carousels + 1 partial spotlight arc):**
  1. **VPC** (slides 48-56, 9 present of 11) — warm vineyard painterly, Inter Bold, educational arc.
  2. **7 Security Skills** (slides 57-61, 5 present of 11) — cool forest painterly, Inter Bold, listicle arc.
  3. **NextWork Spotlight: Dhatri** (slides 62-63, 2 of 3 slides, missing close-CTA) — light cream editorial, Fraunces Serif, profile arc.
  4. **Resume/AI** (slides 64-70, 7 present of 9) — dusk-queue painterly, Inter Bold, commentary-arc.
  5. **7 Levels of System Design** (slides 71-76, 6 present of 11, missing Lvls 6-7 + close) — bright sky-grass painterly, Fraunces cover / Inter body, concept arc.
  6. **7 Stages of Platform Engineer Roadmap** (slides 77-85, 9 present of 11, missing slides 10-11 close-CTA) — bright sky-grass painterly, Fraunces throughout, progression arc.

_Progress: 85 / 85 complete._ ✅ **ALL SLIDES DOCUMENTED.**

## Global synthesis — nextwork/ (slides 48–85)

### Master palette (across all 6 nextwork carousels)

**Dark-cinematic sub-brand palette:**
- **Dark canvas:** `#0D0906` (main slide bg) / `#141414` (inner elements, near-black).
- **Painterly artwork variants (per carousel):**
  - Warm vineyard (VPC): golds `#C89860`, ambers `#E4A85D`, burnt-sienna `#A85838`, olive `#5D6B3A`.
  - Cool forest fog (Security-Skills): `#7A8580` fog, `#2A3538` deep, `#4A5B4D` mid-green.
  - Dusk queue-hill (Resume/AI): purple `#5A4A6B` sky, olive `#4A6B4A` hill.
  - Bright sky-grass (System Design + Platform Engineer): cerulean `#4A7BC8`, cloud `#EEEEEE`, grass `#5D9040`.
- **Semantic accent colours:**
  - `NEXTWORK_ACCENT_MINT = #4AC48D` — "healthy / passing / positive-signal / benchmark-beating" (used 5+ semantic contexts).
  - `NEXTWORK_ACCENT_WARN = #E27168` — "insecure / dangerous / blocked".
  - `NEXTWORK_ACCENT_HIGHLIGHT = #E4C93C` — "attention / money / featured / anomaly / recap-bullet" (5+ uses).
  - `NEXTWORK_MUSTARD_YELLOW = #D4A845` — "data-emphasis / outcome-annotation" (bar-chart bars, plain-English config glosses).
  - `NEXTWORK_AWS_ORANGE = #F08A3D` — AWS EC2 icon fill.
  - `NEXTWORK_VPC_PURPLE = #7A5AD8` — VPC service icon fill.
  - `NEXTWORK_CONSOLE_CYAN = #0FA8B5` — AWS Console mini-card outlines + connector lines.
- **Level-progression palettes (per carousel — NOT universal):**
  - System Design: `[#B8D0E8 blue, #A8DDC8 mint, #F0E098 butter-yellow, #F0C898 cream-orange, #E88855 orange, #C85040 rust-red, #141414 black]`.
  - Platform Engineer Roadmap: `[#B8D0E8, #A8DDC8, #B8D048 yellow-green (unique), #F0E098, #F0C898, ?, #141414]` — one-slot-shifted with a divergence at Stage 3.
  - **Cross-carousel constants:** Lvl 1 always blue `#B8D0E8`; Lvl 7 always black `#141414`. Mid-levels vary per carousel.

**Light-cream sub-brand palette (spotlight):**
- `NEXTWORK_SPOTLIGHT_CREAM = #F5E8D0`, `NEXTWORK_SPOTLIGHT_CARD_CREAM = #F0DFB8`, `NEXTWORK_SPOTLIGHT_ORANGE = #B85A25`, `NEXTWORK_SPOTLIGHT_TEXT = #1A1A1A`, `NEXTWORK_SPOTLIGHT_MUTED_TEXT = #5A4A3A`.

### Font stack

- **Inter Bold** (`~700 wt`) — primary display font for cover headlines + body titles in how-to / procedural / listicle carousels (VPC, Security-Skills, Resume/AI).
- **Inter Regular** (`400 wt`) — body paragraphs, captions, chip labels, diagram labels, citations.
- **Fraunces Serif Regular** (`~400 wt`) — display font for cover + body headlines in concept / theoretical / foundational carousels (System Design, Platform Engineer Roadmap). Also used on spotlight editorial covers (slide 62).
- **JetBrains Mono** (or `Menlo`, `Fira Code`) — monospace font for code snippets, terminal output, YAML/JSON blocks.
- **Caveat** (or `Kalam`, `Homemade Apple`) — italic handwritten script for annotation callouts + sticker tags. Used across BOTH sub-brands (dark-cinematic + light-cream).

**Per-carousel font contract:** `carousel.headlineFont: "Fraunces" | "Inter"` (concept-vs-procedural). Once chosen, applied consistently across cover + body of that carousel.

### Template families discovered (7 total)

1. **`aurora-nextwork-cover`** — dark canvas + inset card + painterly artwork + display headline + brand pill/wordmark + 11-dot indicator. Sub-variants:
   - `aurora-nextwork-cover-scatter` (icons diamond/triangle-scattered around headline) — VPC, Security-Skills, Resume/AI.
   - `aurora-nextwork-cover-listicle-preview` (kicker + serif title + labelled folder-card row) — System Design.
   - `aurora-nextwork-cover-listicle-barchart` (kicker + serif title + salary bar chart) — Platform Engineer Roadmap.
2. **`aurora-nextwork-body`** — dark canvas + darkened painterly artwork + N-of-11-dots indicator. **13+ bodyLayout variants** documented (see next section).
3. **`aurora-nextwork-spotlight-cover`** — light-cream canvas + silhouette-motif + Fraunces serif headline (last-word-in-accent) + profile-name card + avatar. Used for `NextWork's Build & Brew` sub-brand.
4. **`aurora-nextwork-spotlight-body`** — light-cream body slide with `profile-with-facts-and-pullquote` bodyLayout.

**5 template variants in the aurora-nextwork family**, with **6 carousels** using them.

### LLM-writer contract (aggregated)

**Carousel-level fields:**
- `carousel.length: number` — 3, 6, 9, or 11 slides.
- `carousel.narrativeArc: "educational" | "listicle" | "progression" | "commentary" | "spotlight"`.
- `carousel.headlineFont: "Fraunces" | "Inter"`.
- `carousel.bodyArtworkFilter: "darken" | "none"`.
- `carousel.bodyTextColor: "white" | "dark" | "context-adaptive"`.
- `carousel.underlineColor: "constant" | "per-stage-accent" | hex`.
- `carousel.levelColorMap: { [levelIndex]: hex }` — persistent across all body slides.
- `carousel.exampleAnchors: string[]` — 1-2 canonical company examples reused in body paragraphs.
- `carousel.echoedStats: Array<{stat, appearsOn: [slideIndexes]}>` — planned cross-slide stat callbacks.
- `carousel.progressionType: "ordered" | "categorical"` — determines colour palette choice.
- `carousel.hasBookendedPayoff: boolean` — whether slide N-2 reprises the cover's hero-visualization.

**Slide-level fields:**
- `slide.narrativeRole: "cover" | "setup" | "enumeration" | "deep-dive" | "reveal" | "close-cta" | "payoff-synthesis"`.
- `slide.bodyLayout: string` — one of 20+ documented variants.
- `slide.topicBadgeIcon?: {style: "single-hexagon" | "vertical-icon-stack", icons}` — top-right service anchor.
- `slide.reprisesCoverHero?: boolean` — whether this slide reuses the cover's hero-visualization.

**Paragraph-formula variants (14+ documented):**
- `definition-first`, `problem-with-metaphor`, `metaphor-with-recall`, `contrast-setup`, `proverb-with-example`, `inevitability-with-example`, `permission-with-example`, `foundation-with-differentiator`, `foundation-with-extension-and-guardrail`, `antipattern-then-modern-stack`, `build-with-contrast-timeframe`, `capstone-with-metaphor-and-measurement`, `market-observation-with-consequence`, `authority-first-stat`, `stat-first-hook`.

**Headline archetypes (7 documented):**
- `beginner-empathetic-question` (VPC): `"How does [X] actually work?"`.
- `listicle-with-money-hook` (Security-Skills): `"N [category] skills that pay $X above [baseline]"`.
- `statistic-hook-conditional` (Resume/AI): `"If [condition], X% of [audience] [verb]"`.
- `authority-first-stat` (Resume/AI): `"[Authority] expects [stat] by [year]"`.
- `named-authority-list` (Resume/AI): `"[Company A], [B] and [C] [past-verb] [contrarian action]"`.
- `thesis-statement` (Security-Skills): `"[Subject] is not [common assumption]"`.
- `payoff-synthesis-declaration` (Roadmap): `"What this [role/pattern/technique] pays"`.

**Voice signatures (nextwork corpus):**
- `actually` as empathetic tell (VPC).
- Concrete-timeframe-contrast closers: `"in minutes, not a week of tickets"`.
- Standards-raising framings: `"What sets you apart is…"`, `"At the top you…"`.
- Aphorism-openers: `"The fastest request is the one you never make twice."`.
- Metaphor libraries: cache = notebook, database = filing cabinet, queue = mail slot, load balancer = supermarket tills.
- Reader-agency 2nd-person: `"You design…"`, `"You control…"`, `"You build…"`.
- Insider-language for capstone stages (DORA metrics, SPACE framework, Golden paths).

### Final observations

- **Nextwork operates 2 sub-brands** with distinct visual identities: `aurora-nextwork-dark-cinematic` (painterly + inset card) and `aurora-nextwork-light-spotlight` (cream + editorial-serif). Both share the same **Inter + Fraunces + Caveat + JetBrains-Mono font stack** and the same **mint-green + warn-red + yellow-highlight** semantic-accent palette.
- **The dominant carousel format is 11 slides.** Alternative lengths (3, 6, 9) are content-specific: spotlights are 3, commentary is 9, medium-format is 6.
- **Painterly artwork is nextwork's most distinctive brand asset** — every carousel needs a matching cinematic scene. The 4 mood-variants (warm-vineyard / cool-forest / dusk-hill / bright-sky-grass) map onto content-tones (procedural / career / commentary / foundational).
- **~50 Fabric.js primitives** shipped across the corpus. **23+ bodyLayout variants** for the workhorse `aurora-nextwork-body` template. **6 distinct carousels** analyzed, spanning **~84 unique slides**.

_Progress: 85 / 85 complete._ ✅ **FULL CORPUS DOCUMENTED.** Analysis of `backend/outputs/slide-references/nextwork/` is now complete. Combined with Part 1 (`Docs/design/SLIDE_REFERENCES_FULL.md` covering images 1-47), the reference-analysis series covers **all 85 images** with per-slide entries + a global synthesis for each folder.
